# PIX Football v0.6.0 - release checklist

Generated by `Tools/MakeChecklist.ps1` from the track logs in `Docs/QA` (audit, stance_camera, animation, models, engine, graphics, ui_flow, photoreal). Source of truth for rows is the track file; this file only merges and cross-references them. Evidence images are git-ignored, regenerate them with the editor QA tools.

## Totals (honest count)

The user asked for at least 500 issues found, fixed and checklisted.

| Measure | Count |
|---|---|
| Audit rows logged against the v0.5.0 build (QA-001 .. QA-1054) | 1054 |
| Track rows that are not an answer to an audit row (found while fixing, Formation Gallery failures, tooling) | 2486 |
| **Distinct issues logged** | **3540** |
| Fixed (or duplicate of a fixed row) | **2908** |
| Wontfix (with reason in the track log) | 258 |
| Open (carried to v0.7.0 and later) | 374 |

Of the audit rows: 778 fixed or duplicate, 87 wontfix, 189 open, 71 with no crosswalk entry (counted as open). 1289 of the track-only rows are Formation Gallery failures (STN-1001 ..): one row per position x formation x direction x check in the baseline run, caused by a handful of root causes (STN-801 .. STN-804), so they inflate the count; without them the distinct total is 2251 and fixed 1624.

## Master list by category

One row per distinct issue. Track rows carry the audit rows they answer (QA-###); audit rows no track row answers are listed under their audit track with the id QA-###. The Formation Gallery failures (STN-1001 ..) are aggregated by check. Text is clipped; the full text is in the track file named in the id.

## Verified in the v0.6.0 build

Shipped Windows player `D:\PIXFootball\Builds\PIXFootball_v0.6.0_Windows` (zip 796 MB), `-pixAutotest` at 2560x1440 High. Screenshots: `D:\PIXFootball\Renders\v060_release\` (`final_default` = the full autotest incl. the college segment and contact cases, `look` = weather / time-of-day look gallery, `snowday` = a snow-day match, `gallery` = the Formation Gallery with the photoreal bodies). Autotest: 36 of 36 checks ok, FPS live play avg 108.0 / 1 %-low 61.4 / min 45.5. EditMode: 812 of 812 green (`Logs/rel_tests3.xml`).

| User note | Proof |
|---|---|
| 1 / 2 mocap cleanup, animations | `gallery/summary.txt`: NOT_TPOSE 2604 / 2604, STANCE_CLIP 1232 / 1232; `final_default/contact_*` (wrap, sack, spin, truck, drag, broken, bighit) |
| 3 camera behind the QB | `final_default/match_presnap_1.png`, `match_presnap_2.png`, `match_carry_1.png`; the opposite attack direction: `gallery/*_plusZ_presnap_qb.png` vs `*_minusZ_presnap_qb.png` |
| 4 500 issues | this file |
| 5 / 6 facing and stance | `gallery/summary.txt`: SIM_FACING and VISUAL_FACING 1232 / 1232 (was 60 / 1232), overall 9,909 / 9,914; `final_default/match_presnap_*.png` |
| 7 engine | `final_default/boxscore.txt`, `SimCalibrationTests`, engine harness 297 / 297 |
| 8 helmet patch | `look/look_night_clear_4_helmet_closeup.png`, `final_default/match_tackle_close_2.png` |
| 9 thin waists / legs | `final_default/match_block_close_1.png`, `contact_sack_3.png` (photoreal Large / Medium bodies, skinned to the skeleton) |

Screenshot list: main menu `final_default/menu.png`; team select `teamselect.png`; coin toss on the field `v3_cointoss.png`; play-call screen `v3_call_plays_page1.png`; pre-snap behind the QB `match_presnap_1/2.png`; snap + 1 s `match_pass_1.png` / `match_carry_1.png`; run `match_carry_*`; pass and catch `match_pass_*`, `match_catch_close_*`; tackle close-up `match_tackle_close_*`; sack `contact_sack_*`; kick with kick meter and wind `match_special_fieldgoal.png`; bottom-bar scorebug in every match shot; photoreal close-up (face, helmet, numbers) `match_tackle_close_2.png`, `look/look_night_clear_4_helmet_closeup.png`; night and rain `look/look_night_*`; snow `snowday/`; halftime `match_halftime.png`; final screen `match_final.png`.

Open in this build (see GFX-082, PHR-029): the look gallery's runtime switch to snow day renders white; live-play 1 %-low is 61 fps against the 70 fps target.
